独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 38089377827 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 3

重要指引缺失上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F2 · 文件插件 Skill 未命名应用侧可按路径拦截插件路由的唯一公共钩子

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file · @nocobase/app-server · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

guidance-gap 成立：file Skill 要求为每个贡献自有路径注册 App 侧认证/授权并按路径限定、禁止 use('*')（E7,E16,E17），但 Skill 与 server-routes.md 都未点名 defineHttpMiddleware/addHttpMiddleware，也未说明插件路由由 addServerPlugins 先于 runtime 路由挂载。app-server 实际公开该钩子（E10,E12）且 registerRoutes 先执行 httpMiddleware 再挂载路由（E13）；/api 贡献在插件 routes 之后加入（E11）。应用只能自读 dist 发现并用 defineHttpMiddleware + addHttpMiddleware 实现守护（E30,E31）。属文档可发现性缺口，非契约违约。

  • 职责与公开约定：file Skill 第128行要求对每个贡献自有路径注册 App 认证/授权并禁止 use('*')，server-routes 只给“每个路由自保”与“限定自有路径”原则（E7,E16,E17）；内容路由公开且 Policy 不覆盖（E1）。约定本身成立。 E7 E16 E17 E1
  • 实际行为证据：app-server 导出 defineHttpMiddleware 与 addHttpMiddleware（E10,E12）；registerRoutes 先执行 httpMiddleware.register 再构造/挂载路由（E13），而插件 routes 在 addServerPlugins 阶段、runtime routes 之前入列（E11）。钩子确实存在且可先于插件路由执行。 E10 E11 E12 E13
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用以 defineHttpMiddleware 定义 materialAccessMiddleware（E30），并在 addRuntimeContributions 前 addHttpMiddleware（E31），正确按 /api/materialFiles/* 与内容路径守护，说明机制可用、只是未被指引点名。 E30 E31
  • 环境因素：挂载顺序由 app.ts 的调用顺序与 registerRoutes 代码决定（E31,E13），与外部服务、凭据、依赖安装无关；环境不能解释该文档缺口。 E31 E13
  • 工厂因素：缺口位于工厂交付的 file Skill 与 app 开发 Skill 指引本身（E7,E16,E17），二者只写原则；未见工厂脚本或模板覆盖导致，故归因为指引缺口。 E7 E16 E17
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：公开入口 defineHttpMiddleware/addHttpMiddleware 与正确的先挂载顺序已存在（E10,E11,E12,E13），正常组合即可满足“插件公开路径路由前鉴权”；缺的是指引而非能力。 E10 E11 E12 E13

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

file 插件的内容字节路由按设计公开且 Policy 不覆盖（E7/E1），Skill 要求“在每个贡献挂载前于其自有路径上注册 App 认证/授权”并禁止 use('*')，但没有说明该钩子是 @nocobase/app-server 的 defineHttpMiddleware + addHttpMiddleware，也没说明插件路由由 addServerPlugins 先于应用路由挂载。server-routes.md 只给了原则。Agent 需读编译产物才能发现唯一可行机制；这是隐私边界相关的文档缺口。

实际影响
开发者按 Skill 无法独立完成“插件公开路径的路由前鉴权”，只能阅读编译产物；隐私边界相关需求的可发现性与可复制性下降。
建议改法
在 file 插件 SKILL.md 与 server-routes.md 写明 defineHttpMiddleware/addHttpMiddleware，并说明插件路由由 addServerPlugins 先于应用路由挂载、应用 router 无法包住它。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
应用或插件贡献的公开路径（含 file 插件内容路由）需要在路由执行前按属主鉴权。
应有能力 / 行为
Skill/参考文档点名 defineHttpMiddleware + addHttpMiddleware，并说明插件路由先挂载导致应用 router 无法包住它。
实际观察
文档只给出“在自有路径注册认证、禁止 use('*')”的原则，未命名公共钩子与挂载顺序。
应用绕行与代价
Agent 从 app-server 编译产物发现 addHttpMiddleware，并用 defineHttpMiddleware 注册 materialAccessMiddleware。
改进后的验收标准
按 Skill 独立可完成同样守卫，无需阅读 dist 源码。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 9 条
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:120–128E7

Skill 说明内容路由 "is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it"，要求 "register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it" 并禁止 use('*')，但未给出应用侧可用的挂载钩子名。

Every exposed action appears in the application's API document at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`), under the exposure's name as its tag with operationIds such as `invoiceAttachmentsFindMany` and `invoiceAttachmentsUploadOne`. The upload operations are documented as `multipart/form-data` with their `maxSize`, `201` result and `400`/`403`/`413`/`415` errors; read it there rather than guessing a request shape. The content route under `accessPath` is outside `/api` and is not in the document.

`policy` is required and governs every action of the exposure, uploads included. It is a Repository Policy: `read`, `create`, `update` and `delete`, each `true`, `false`, or a rule node. A node that is `false` refuses that operation; a node with no `fields` accepts no caller-supplied field, which is what a metadata `createOne` needs before it will accept anything. Pass a function of the principal — `policy: (principal) => ({ ... })` — together with a `principal(context)` resolver to scope rows to the caller; without a principal the request is refused with 403.

An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

Content is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:26–54E1

defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes 标注 "Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned."，并校验 accessPath 形态/冲突与 disk 必填，把内容路由的所有权边界显式交给应用。

/** Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned. */
export function defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes(options) {
    const paths = [];
    const entries = options.repositories.map((entry) => {
        const accessPath = normalizeAccessPath(entry.accessPath ?? `/uploads/${entry.name}`);
        if (paths.some((path) => path === accessPath ||
            path.startsWith(`${accessPath}/`) ||
            accessPath.startsWith(`${path}/`)))
            throw new Error(`Conflicting file accessPath: ${accessPath}`);
        paths.push(accessPath);
        if (!entry.disk)
            throw new Error('File repository disk is required.');
        if (entry.accessMode !== undefined &&
            !['stream', 'redirect'].includes(entry.accessMode))
            throw new Error('Invalid file accessMode.');
        const { uploadOne, uploadMany, ...actions } = entry.actions;
        for (const upload of [uploadOne, uploadMany]) {
            if (upload === undefined)
                continue;
            if (!upload ||
                typeof upload !== 'object' ||
                Object.keys(upload).some((key) => key !== 'maxSize'))
                throw new Error('Invalid upload action configuration.');
            if (upload.maxSize !== undefined &&
                (!Number.isSafeInteger(upload.maxSize) || upload.maxSize <= 0))
                throw new Error('maxSize must be a positive safe integer.');
        }
        return { ...entry, accessPath, actions, uploadOne, uploadMany };
    });
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:72–78E16

"Every route owns its own security"：挂载到 /api 不认证任何请求，auth.required() 拒绝匿名，要求每个路由自行保护。

## Every route owns its own security

**Mounting under `/api` does not authenticate anything.** `/api` is a location. A route with no `auth.required()` is public regardless of where it mounts.

Never depend on middleware installed by another route, or on the order contributions happen to be registered in. Contribution order changes when a plugin is added, and a route protected only by someone else's middleware silently becomes public.

`auth.required()` rejects anonymous requests with `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`, reason `AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED`. `auth.optional()` attaches the session when present without rejecting.
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:175–177E17

"Scope middleware to paths you own"：禁止顶层 router.use('*', ...)，主张显式路径或隔离子路由。

## Scope middleware to paths you own

Use the explicit path, or an isolated sub-router mounted at your prefix as above. A `router.use('*', ...)` on the top-level router leaks into contributions mounted later and is the way an unrelated endpoint accidentally becomes protected — or, worse, the way yours accidentally is not.
packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/routes.js:1–6E10

defineHttpMiddleware 返回冻结的 {name, register}，是 app-server 公开的 HTTP 中间件定义入口。

export function defineHttpMiddleware(options) {
    return Object.freeze({
        name: options.name,
        register: options.register,
    });
}
packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:155–162E12

addRoutes 追加路由贡献，addHttpMiddleware 把中间件压入 this.httpMiddleware，是应用可用的路由前拦截注册点。

    addRoutes(routes, options = {}) {
        this.assertRoutesMutable();
        this.routes.push({ contribution: routes, owner: options.owner });
    }
    addHttpMiddleware(middleware) {
        this.assertRoutesMutable();
        this.httpMiddleware.push(middleware);
    }
packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:245–265E13

registerRoutes 先逐个执行 httpMiddleware.register(router,this)，再构造并挂载 /api 子路由与根路由；因此该处注册的守卫对插件路由同样先执行。

    async registerRoutes() {
        if (this.routesRegistered) {
            return;
        }
        for (const middleware of this.httpMiddleware) {
            await middleware.register(this.router, this);
        }
        // Every API contribution mounts into one router so `/api` answers errors and unknown paths in the standard error
        // body. It mounts before the root contributions, whose catch-alls (the SPA's `/*`) would otherwise answer an
        // unknown API path with a page.
        const api = new Hono();
        api.use('*', requestIdMiddleware());
        // The `api` section's global limits, each installed only when configured.
        installApiLimits(api, this.config.get('api'));
        // The last resort: anything no router recognized is an unexpected failure, answered with an opaque 500.
        api.onError((error, context) => apiErrorHandler(toApiError(error), context));
        const apiRouters = [];
        const roots = [];
        for (const [index, { contribution, owner }] of this.routes.entries()) {
            const router = await contribution.createRouter(this);
            if (contribution.scope === 'api') {
packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:114–147E11

addServerPlugins 在插件注册阶段就把 plugin.definition.routes 加入 this.routes，随后 addRuntimeContributions 才加入 runtime.routes：应用 route 无法包住先挂载的插件路由。

    addServerPlugins(serverPlugins) {
        this.appPackageName = serverPlugins.appPackageName;
        this.databaseTaskContributionsValue =
            createAppDatabaseTaskContributions(serverPlugins);
        for (const plugin of serverPlugins.plugins) {
            for (const Provider of plugin.definition.serviceProviders) {
                this.addServiceProvider(Provider);
            }
            for (const routes of plugin.definition.routes) {
                this.addRoutes(routes, { owner: plugin.definition.packageName });
            }
            if (plugin.definition.queue) {
                this.queueJobPlugins.push(plugin.definition.packageName);
            }
            if (plugin.definition.locales) {
                this.localeContributions.push({
                    packageName: plugin.definition.packageName,
                    locales: plugin.definition.locales,
                });
            }
        }
    }
    addRuntimeContributions(runtime) {
        this.addServerPlugins(runtime.plugins);
        if (runtime.locales) {
            this.addApplicationLocales(runtime.locales);
        }
        this.addServiceProviders(runtime.serviceProviders);
        for (const routes of runtime.routes) {
            this.addRoutes(routes, {
                owner: this.appPackageName ?? 'the application',
            });
        }
    }
app/server/middleware/material-access.ts:28–71E30

应用中间件用 defineHttpMiddleware：对 /api/materialFiles/* 要求登录；对 <accessPath>/:file 读 session 并核对 ownerId，匿名 401、他人 404，隐藏记录是否存在。

export const materialAccessMiddleware = defineHttpMiddleware<Application>({
  name: '@nocobase/app/materials/access',
  register(router, app) {
    // An application assembled without the authentication plugin has nothing to guard; the routes it owns are not
    // mounted either. Skipping keeps such an assembly, which the runtime tests build, startable.
    if (!app.container.has(authenticationToken)) return;
    const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);

    router.use(
      '/api/materialFiles/*',
      auth.required() as unknown as MiddlewareHandler,
    );

    router.use(`${MATERIAL_FILES_ACCESS_PATH}/:file`, (async (
      context: Context,
      next: () => Promise<void>,
    ) => {
      const session = await readSession(auth, context.req.raw.headers);
      const ownerId = session?.user
        ? await findFileOwner(app, context.req.param('file'))
        : undefined;
      if (!session?.user || ownerId !== session.user.id) {
        return apiErrorHandler(
          new ApiError(
            session?.user
              ? {
                  status: 'NOT_FOUND',
                  reason: 'MATERIAL_FILE_NOT_FOUND',
                  domain: 'materials',
                  message: 'File not found.',
                }
              : {
                  status: 'UNAUTHENTICATED',
                  reason: 'AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED',
                  domain: 'authentication',
                  message: 'Authentication required.',
                },
          ),
          context,
        );
      }
      await next();
    }) as unknown as MiddlewareHandler);
  },
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [重要][指引缺失] 文件插件 Skill 未命名应用侧可按路径拦截插件路由的唯一公共钩子

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-file`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.18）、`packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file`、`@nocobase/app-server`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.0）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

guidance-gap 成立：file Skill 要求为每个贡献自有路径注册 App 侧认证/授权并按路径限定、禁止 use('*')（E7,E16,E17），但 Skill 与 server-routes.md 都未点名 defineHttpMiddleware/addHttpMiddleware，也未说明插件路由由 addServerPlugins 先于 runtime 路由挂载。app-server 实际公开该钩子（E10,E12）且 registerRoutes 先执行 httpMiddleware 再挂载路由（E13）；/api 贡献在插件 routes 之后加入（E11）。应用只能自读 dist 发现并用 defineHttpMiddleware + addHttpMiddleware 实现守护（E30,E31）。属文档可发现性缺口，非契约违约。

- 职责与公开约定：file Skill 第128行要求对每个贡献自有路径注册 App 认证/授权并禁止 use('*')，server-routes 只给“每个路由自保”与“限定自有路径”原则（E7,E16,E17）；内容路由公开且 Policy 不覆盖（E1）。约定本身成立。；证据：E7（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:120–128）、E16（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:72–78）、E17（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:175–177）、E1（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:26–54）
- 实际行为证据：app-server 导出 defineHttpMiddleware 与 addHttpMiddleware（E10,E12）；registerRoutes 先执行 httpMiddleware.register 再构造/挂载路由（E13），而插件 routes 在 addServerPlugins 阶段、runtime routes 之前入列（E11）。钩子确实存在且可先于插件路由执行。；证据：E10（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/routes.js:1–6）、E11（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:114–147）、E12（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:155–162）、E13（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:245–265）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用以 defineHttpMiddleware 定义 materialAccessMiddleware（E30），并在 addRuntimeContributions 前 addHttpMiddleware（E31），正确按 /api/materialFiles/* 与内容路径守护，说明机制可用、只是未被指引点名。；证据：E30（app/server/middleware/material-access.ts:28–71）、E31（app/server/app.ts:42–49）
- 环境因素：挂载顺序由 app.ts 的调用顺序与 registerRoutes 代码决定（E31,E13），与外部服务、凭据、依赖安装无关；环境不能解释该文档缺口。；证据：E31（app/server/app.ts:42–49）、E13（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:245–265）
- 工厂因素：缺口位于工厂交付的 file Skill 与 app 开发 Skill 指引本身（E7,E16,E17），二者只写原则；未见工厂脚本或模板覆盖导致，故归因为指引缺口。；证据：E7（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:120–128）、E16（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:72–78）、E17（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:175–177）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：公开入口 defineHttpMiddleware/addHttpMiddleware 与正确的先挂载顺序已存在（E10,E11,E12,E13），正常组合即可满足“插件公开路径路由前鉴权”；缺的是指引而非能力。；证据：E10（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/routes.js:1–6）、E11（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:114–147）、E12（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:155–162）、E13（packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:245–265）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

file 插件的内容字节路由按设计公开且 Policy 不覆盖（E7/E1），Skill 要求“在每个贡献挂载前于其自有路径上注册 App 认证/授权”并禁止 use('*')，但没有说明该钩子是 @nocobase/app-server 的 defineHttpMiddleware + addHttpMiddleware，也没说明插件路由由 addServerPlugins 先于应用路由挂载。server-routes.md 只给了原则。Agent 需读编译产物才能发现唯一可行机制；这是隐私边界相关的文档缺口。

**影响**：开发者按 Skill 无法独立完成“插件公开路径的路由前鉴权”，只能阅读编译产物；隐私边界相关需求的可发现性与可复制性下降。

**建议改动**：在 file 插件 SKILL.md 与 server-routes.md 写明 defineHttpMiddleware/addHttpMiddleware，并说明插件路由由 addServerPlugins 先于应用路由挂载、应用 router 无法包住它。

**验收标准**：按 Skill 独立可完成同样守卫，无需阅读 dist 源码。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:120–128` — Skill 说明内容路由 "is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it"，要求 "register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it" 并禁止 use('*')，但未给出应用侧可用的挂载钩子名。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:26–54` — defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes 标注 "Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned."，并校验 accessPath 形态/冲突与 disk 必填，把内容路由的所有权边界显式交给应用。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:72–78` — "Every route owns its own security"：挂载到 /api 不认证任何请求，auth.required() 拒绝匿名，要求每个路由自行保护。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:175–177` — "Scope middleware to paths you own"：禁止顶层 router.use('*', ...)，主张显式路径或隔离子路由。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/routes.js:1–6` — defineHttpMiddleware 返回冻结的 {name, register}，是 app-server 公开的 HTTP 中间件定义入口。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:155–162` — addRoutes 追加路由贡献，addHttpMiddleware 把中间件压入 this.httpMiddleware，是应用可用的路由前拦截注册点。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:245–265` — registerRoutes 先逐个执行 httpMiddleware.register(router,this)，再构造并挂载 /api 子路由与根路由；因此该处注册的守卫对插件路由同样先执行。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/application/index.js:114–147` — addServerPlugins 在插件注册阶段就把 plugin.definition.routes 加入 this.routes，随后 addRuntimeContributions 才加入 runtime.routes：应用 route 无法包住先挂载的插件路由。
- `app/server/middleware/material-access.ts:28–71` — 应用中间件用 defineHttpMiddleware：对 /api/materialFiles/* 要求登录；对 <accessPath>/:file 读 session 并核对 ownerId，匿名 401、他人 404，隐藏记录是否存在。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 38089377827 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
轻微易用性改进上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F1 · Registry 预览组件对损坏的位图/PDF/媒体没有加载失败状态

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

usability-improvement 成立：缺省 Registry 的 image/pdf/audio/video 分支直接渲染媒体元素且无 onError，FileThumbnail 亦为裸 <img>（E5,E6）；README 仅承诺 OOXML 失败提示（E9）。应用复制源并新增 ImagePreview/files.imageFailed 后才在 B06 给出明确提示（E35,E39）。属缺省组件的可用性改进空间，不是契约违约。

  • 职责与公开约定：README 承诺预览覆盖 raster/PDF/音视频，并只写明 OOXML failures show an error（E9）；组件以 error 属性接收调用方传入的读取错误（E5）。未承诺位图/媒体自身的加载失败态，故可作改进。 E9 E5
  • 实际行为证据：E5 中 image/pdf/audio/video 直接渲染 <img>/<iframe>/<audio>/<video>，无 onError；E6 FileThumbnail 为裸 <img> 无 onError；应用复制版新增 ImagePreview onError 与 files.imageFailed（E35）。 E5 E6 E35
  • 应用接入与业务边界：B06 依赖应用改动：复制 Registry 源并加 onError 后损坏图片显示明确提示（E35,E39），说明缺省组件本身不满足“可读失败提示”。 E35 E39
  • 环境因素：损坏 PNG 的失败发生在浏览器解码，根因是组件缺少 onError，与外部服务、凭据、依赖安装无关（E5,E35）。 E5 E35
  • 工厂因素：缺省预览源与 README 由插件 Registry 交付、应用可复制编辑（E5,E6,E9）；缺口在交付源自身，未见工厂脚本或模板覆盖导致。 E5 E6 E9
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：FilePreviewContent 已有 error 属性但只接调用方传入的读取错误（E5），媒体元素无自身 onError；App 可改复制源，但缺省 Registry 不提供该失败态（E9,E35）。 E5 E9 E35

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

file-preview-content.tsx 与 file-thumbnail.tsx 对 image/pdf/audio/video 直接渲染 <img>/<iframe>/<audio>/<video>，没有 onError 或占位失败态；只有 office/OOXML 分支有失败回退。README 只承诺 OOXML 失败提示，因此损坏 PNG 在缺省 Registry 中只会显示浏览器破图，B06 依赖 Agent 修改已复制的 App 源（新增 ImagePreview/files.imageFailed）才给出明确提示。

实际影响
缺省预览在损坏图片/PDF/媒体上只显示浏览器破图或空白，用户无法区分“文件损坏”与“加载失败”，需应用自行补丁才能满足“明确失败提示”。
建议改法
在 image/pdf/audio/video 分支增加 onError 与可本地化的失败占位提示（如 files.imageFailed），并同步更新 README 的覆盖说明。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
为 Registry 预览提供损坏或不可解码的图片/PDF/音视频，且宿主应用未自行补失败态。
应有能力 / 行为
缺省预览组件对 load error 给出可读失败提示，而不是浏览器破图或空白。
实际观察
image/pdf/audio/video 分支无失败态；仅 OOXML 有 error 回退，B06 由应用侧改动满足。
应用绕行与代价
复制 Registry 源并在自身副本补充 onError 失败态（本次即如此）。
改进后的验收标准
修复后损坏 PNG 在未修改缺省组件时也应显示明确失败提示。

  if (error)
    return (
      <div role='alert'>{t(messageKey(error), { defaultValue: error })}</div>
    );

        {t('files.loadingPreview', { defaultValue: 'Loading preview...' })}

        <img
          src={url}
          alt={file.filename}
          className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full object-contain'
        />
      );
    case 'pdf':
      return (
        <iframe title={file.filename} src={url} className='h-[70vh] w-full' />
      );
    case 'audio':
      return <audio controls src={url} className='w-full' />;
    case 'video':
      return <video controls src={url} className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full' />;
    case 'markdown':
      return <MarkdownPreview text={text} />;
    case 'text':
      return (
        <pre className='max-h-[70vh] overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-md bg-muted p-3 text-sm'>
          {text ??
            t('files.loadingPreview', { defaultValue: 'Loading preview...' })}
        </pre>
      );
    default:
      return <DownloadFallback file={file} onDownload={onDownload} />;

export function FileThumbnail({
  file,
  url,
  alt = file.filename,
}: FileThumbnailProps): ReactElement {
  const imageUrl = resolveSafeFileUrl(
    url ?? (isSafeImagePreview(file) ? (file.contentUrl ?? '') : ''),
  );
  return imageUrl ? (
    <img
      data-slot='file-thumbnail'
      src={imageUrl}
      alt={alt}
      className='h-full w-full object-cover'
    />
  ) : (
    <span
      data-slot='file-thumbnail'
      aria-label={file.filename}
      className='flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-muted/50 p-3 text-muted-foreground'
    >
      {icon(file)}
    </span>
  );
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/README.md:9–13E9

README 声明预览覆盖 raster/PDF/音视频/文本与本地 OOXML、"OOXML failures show an error"，并明确 "A private disk does not authorize a content request" 且无 public/private 记录标记。

Preview handles safe raster images, PDF through a fetched local blob, audio/video and text/Markdown. DOCX/XLSX/PPTX render locally with lazily loaded `@silurus/ooxml`; legacy Office and OpenDocument formats retain Office Online, which requires an internet-accessible absolute URL and cannot use the App session. Active HTML/SVG/XML and unsafe URL schemes are rejected. Markdown does not execute raw HTML.

Follow the plugin Skill's Registry installation and preview sections for the exact viewer dependency, Vite dependency-prebundling exclusion, authentication adapter boundaries and acceptance checks. Same-origin content fetches use session cookies; external fetches omit credentials and require CORS. Bearer-only access requires changes to the App-owned source; setting `contentUrl` to an arbitrary blob URL is not supported. OOXML failures show an error and an optional download action; `download={false}` suppresses that action.

There is no public/private record flag or access-token endpoint. The item contains no extension.ts, route, collection, or Server implementation. A private disk does not authorize a content request. Installed copies belong to the App; review upgrades with a three-way merge and preserve customizations. Supply translated labels and adapt additional copy in installed source.
app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:102–135E35

应用复制的 Registry 预览源新增 ImagePreview：onError 时显示 files.imageFailed 明确提示，正是 B06 通过所需的应用侧改动。

function ImagePreview(inputProps: {
  readonly file: FileRecord;
  readonly url?: string;
  readonly onDownload?: () => void;
}): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation('@nocobase/app-plugin-file');
  const { file, url, onDownload } = inputProps;
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  if (!url || failed) {
    return (
      <div role='alert'>
        <DownloadFallback
          file={file}
          message={t('files.imageFailed', {
            defaultValue:
              'This image could not be displayed. The file may be corrupt or in a format the browser cannot show.',
          })}
          onDownload={onDownload}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <img
      src={url}
      alt={file.filename}
      className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full object-contain'
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
}

- 应用接入与业务边界：B06 依赖应用改动：复制 Registry 源并加 onError 后损坏图片显示明确提示（E35,E39），说明缺省组件本身不满足“可读失败提示”。；证据：E35（app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:102–135）、E39（artifacts/verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json:88–133）
- 环境因素：损坏 PNG 的失败发生在浏览器解码，根因是组件缺少 onError，与外部服务、凭据、依赖安装无关（E5,E35）。；证据：E5（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:61–98）、E35（app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:102–135）
- 工厂因素：缺省预览源与 README 由插件 Registry 交付、应用可复制编辑（E5,E6,E9）；缺口在交付源自身，未见工厂脚本或模板覆盖导致。；证据：E5（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:61–98）、E6（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/file-thumbnail.tsx:43–66）、E9（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/README.md:9–13）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：FilePreviewContent 已有 error 属性但只接调用方传入的读取错误（E5），媒体元素无自身 onError；App 可改复制源，但缺省 Registry 不提供该失败态（E9,E35）。；证据：E5（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:61–98）、E9（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/README.md:9–13）、E35（app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:102–135）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

file-preview-content.tsx 与 file-thumbnail.tsx 对 image/pdf/audio/video 直接渲染 <img>/<iframe>/<audio>/<video>，没有 onError 或占位失败态；只有 office/OOXML 分支有失败回退。README 只承诺 OOXML 失败提示，因此损坏 PNG 在缺省 Registry 中只会显示浏览器破图，B06 依赖 Agent 修改已复制的 App 源（新增 ImagePreview/files.imageFailed）才给出明确提示。

**影响**：缺省预览在损坏图片/PDF/媒体上只显示浏览器破图或空白，用户无法区分“文件损坏”与“加载失败”，需应用自行补丁才能满足“明确失败提示”。

**建议改动**：在 image/pdf/audio/video 分支增加 onError 与可本地化的失败占位提示（如 files.imageFailed），并同步更新 README 的覆盖说明。

**验收标准**：修复后损坏 PNG 在未修改缺省组件时也应显示明确失败提示。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:61–98` — 预览分派对 image/pdf/audio/video 直接渲染 img/iframe/audio/video，无 onError 或失败态；仅 office/OOXML 分支有失败回退（第 61-64 行的 error 分支只处理传入的 error 属性）。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/file-thumbnail.tsx:43–66` — FileThumbnail 在可安全预览时渲染裸 <img>，无 onError；损坏图片只显示浏览器破图占位。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/README.md:9–13` — README 声明预览覆盖 raster/PDF/音视频/文本与本地 OOXML、"OOXML failures show an error"，并明确 "A private disk does not authorize a content request" 且无 public/private 记录标记。
- `app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:102–135` — 应用复制的 Registry 预览源新增 ImagePreview：onError 时显示 files.imageFailed 明确提示，正是 B06 通过所需的应用侧改动。
- `artifacts/verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json:88–133` — verify-3 的 B05/B06：匿名访问附件 401 UNAUTHENTICATED、同事 404 MATERIAL_FILE_NOT_FOUND、甲仍可读；损坏 PNG 预览给出 "This image could not be displayed..." 明确提示。

  • 实际行为证据：快照中 app/tests/logic/client-shell.test.tsx 已将 data-state 断言包在 waitFor（E37），属补丁后状态；缺原始失败断言与运行输出证实模板确有此缺陷。 E37 E43

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

retro 记录 client-shell.test.tsx 的侧边栏折叠断言在全量运行中偶发失败而在隔离运行/15 次重复中通过；useSidebarPreference 经自定义 DOM 事件同步更新，点击后的同步断言可能早于 React flush。作者已用 waitFor 包裹断言（不削弱断言）。快照不含 @nocobase/app-template-default 源，无法独立复现原始测试，故为 suspected。

实际影响
全量测试偶发失败会触发不必要的修复轮次并消耗验证预算，也可能掩盖真实回归。
建议改法
在模板的侧边栏折叠测试中始终以 waitFor 断言 data-state（本次补丁已如此），并在测试说明中记录共享 DOM 事件的异步性质。

本条未提供结构化触发条件、绕行与回归标准，请按原文和证据复核，不自动补造。

评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
app/client/layouts/use-sidebar-preference.ts:19–41E36

模板的 useSidebarPreference 通过 localStorage + 自定义 DOM 事件共享状态，setCollapsed 同步 dispatchEvent；点击后断言可能早于 React flush。

function subscribe(notify: () => void): () => void {
  const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {
    if (event.key === storageKey || event.key === null) notify();
  };
  window.addEventListener('storage', onStorage);
  window.addEventListener(changeEvent, notify);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('storage', onStorage);
    window.removeEventListener(changeEvent, notify);
  };
}

function setCollapsed(update: SetStateAction<boolean>): void {
  const current = getSnapshot();
  memoryValue = typeof update === 'function' ? update(current) : update;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(storageKey, String(memoryValue));
  } catch {
    memoryOnly = true;
    // The toggle remains usable when storage is blocked.
  }
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(changeEvent));
}
app/tests/logic/client-shell.test.tsx:203–216E37

client-shell.test.tsx 的侧边栏折叠断言已包在 waitFor 中，即作者为消除间歇失败所做的模板改动。

    const sidebar = await screen.findByRole('complementary', {
      name: 'Application navigation',
    });

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Collapse navigation' }),
    );
    // The shared preference notifies React through a DOM event; wait for that update to reach the DOM rather than
    // assuming the click flushed it in the same tick.
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(sidebar.closest('[data-state]')).toHaveAttribute(
        'data-state',
        'collapsed',
      ),

实现者过程与运行背景 · 4 个过程问题 / 2 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

The recorded failure was a flaky shell test, but the underlying defect was that the application still had no implementation of the requested feature: both earlier attempts produced only a scratch script (make-samples.mjs) and no application code. This attempt implemented the whole 'materials and private attachments' feature on the template: a migration for materials/material_files, a materials service and declared /api routes, a file-plugin exposure for uploads, an httpMiddleware that gates the plugin's upload and byte routes by owner, three client pages (list/create/detail) with routes and locales, a seed with two isolated accounts and two sample materials, and a business integration test (tests/logic/materials-api.test.ts). The flaky sidebar assertion was hardened with waitFor without weakening it. Full suite (42 files, 246 tests), eslint, prettier and both tsc projects pass locally; the server build also succeeds and verifies its production dependencies.

处理结果未提供

未提供问题现象

原因（实现者自述）
未提供
处理方式
Registered the guard as app.addHttpMiddleware() in server/app.ts (material-access middleware), which runs before plugin routes. It returns early when authenticationToken is not registered so authentication-free assemblies still start.

问题 03 ·

处理结果未提供

未提供问题现象

原因（实现者自述）
未提供
处理方式
Cookie writes require an Origin/Referer matching app.publicOrigin (APP_PUBLIC_ORIGIN). pnpm dev sets it; the integration test sets APP_PUBLIC_ORIGIN and sends the origin header. Backend Origin checks were not disabled.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 04 ·

处理结果未提供

未提供问题现象

原因（实现者自述）
未提供
处理方式
The sidebar preference is shared through a DOM event, so the post-click assertion could observe the DOM before React flushed. Wrapped both assertions in waitFor, which asserts the same data-state values and only allows the update to arrive.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

本次场景中，文件插件的 Repository/Policy/内容路由、app-server 的 HTTP 中间件挂载与 authentication 的 required()/getSession() 组合，足以在应用侧建立资料私有附件的访问边界（B05 通过），db 的 migration/Builder/Repository 也直接支撑了数据模型、所有者作用域与 B03/B04 的失败语义。主要不足有两点：文件插件生成的 <accessPath> 字节路由按设计公开，但其 Skill 只要求“在挂载前注册 App 认证”而未点名唯一可用的 defineHttpMiddleware/addHttpMiddleware，也未说明插件路由先于应用路由挂载，Agent 只能从编译产物发现；Registry 缺省预览对损坏的位图/PDF/媒体没有失败态，B06 需 Agent 修改已复制的 App 源才给出明确提示。此外模板的侧边栏测试存在间歇性失败（suspected，属模板改进项），首次 QA 因应用未实现而 B01-B06 全失败，属应用侧缺失而非框架缺陷。

本 Run 验证轮次3
本 Run 工厂修复轮次2
已保留验证轮次2

仅本 Run 已归档记录；工厂修复轮次不等于 Agent 开发中全部试错次数。缺少 verify-1 时不能声称任务首轮通过。

首轮与修复过程 · 查看逐轮原始状态
轮次范围原始结论失败项
验证 1全量浏览器 QA报告未通过B01 甲上传 PNG 并保存资料，刷新后能看到真实图片，并能下载打开。
B02 甲上传 DOCX 并保存，刷新后在应用内看到文档正文，而非只有文件名或下载按钮。
B03 先上传文件但留空标题，保存应失败且已选附件仍保留；补上标题后能直接保存。
B04 移除一个附件并保存，刷新后这份资料不再显示该附件。
B05 乙和未登录访问者分别打开甲的资料或附件链接，均不能看到内容；甲仍可正常查看。
B06 使用损坏 PNG，上传或预览实际发现问题时给出明确失败提示；上传已拒绝时不必继续预览。
验证 3全量浏览器 QA报告通过未记录 failed 项；不代表全部操作完成

未提供额外说明。

Infrastructure feedback

做得好的地方与证据

值得保留的框架能力、评审覆盖范围与全部原始证据。原评审置信度与反馈复核结果分别展示，均不代替人工核验。

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 2

做得好的地方
F3 · 策略 + 认证 + 路由前中间件组合出可用的私有附件边界

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · @nocobase/app-server · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md

⌄

file 插件把内容路由声明为公开并把所有权交给应用，app-server 在 /api 与根路由前执行 httpMiddleware，authentication 的 required()/getSession() 返回标准 Hono 中间件——三者组合让应用能用不到百行的中间件实现匿名 401、非属主 404、属主可读，且无需修改框架。认证 Skill 还写明用户名允许字符集（连字符不在内），因此过程中出现的 422 属未遵循已写明约束，不是文档缺口。

提供的帮助
证明无需修改框架即可实现按属主的私有字节访问，降低对自研网关或绕过插件的依赖。
应保留的能力
保持该组合（策略 + 认证 + httpMiddleware）作为私有附件的推荐范式，并在文档中把它写成可照抄配方。

本次评审的覆盖限制
  • 跨页面视觉一致性未评审：模型无法读取图像，ui.status=not-reviewed、score=null；截图仅作为 QA 证据。
  • 仅评审本场景直接涉及的 4 个能力单元；未系统覆盖授权、用户、i18n、工作流等包。
  • verify-1 的 B01-B06 全失败源于应用当时尚无实现（默认空模板），属应用缺失，不据此推断框架问题。
  • F5 与用户名约束的结论部分依赖 retro 自述及生成的测试文件；快照不含模板包源码，故 F5 标记 suspected。
  • historyReview 引用了全部 5 次 invoked 调用的日志分块并逐条处置 98 条显式错误信号，但显式错误数为 0 的 verify-1 不构成“过程顺利”的证据。
  • 快照未包含 3 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。

E1 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:26–54

defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes 标注 "Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned."，并校验 accessPath 形态/冲突与 disk 必填，把内容路由的所有权边界显式交给应用。

/** Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned. */
export function defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes(options) {
    const paths = [];
    const entries = options.repositories.map((entry) => {
        const accessPath = normalizeAccessPath(entry.accessPath ?? `/uploads/${entry.name}`);
        if (paths.some((path) => path === accessPath ||
            path.startsWith(`${accessPath}/`) ||
            accessPath.startsWith(`${path}/`)))
            throw new Error(`Conflicting file accessPath: ${accessPath}`);
        paths.push(accessPath);
        if (!entry.disk)
            throw new Error('File repository disk is required.');
        if (entry.accessMode !== undefined &&
            !['stream', 'redirect'].includes(entry.accessMode))
            throw new Error('Invalid file accessMode.');
        const { uploadOne, uploadMany, ...actions } = entry.actions;
        for (const upload of [uploadOne, uploadMany]) {
            if (upload === undefined)
                continue;
            if (!upload ||
                typeof upload !== 'object' ||
                Object.keys(upload).some((key) => key !== 'maxSize'))
                throw new Error('Invalid upload action configuration.');
            if (upload.maxSize !== undefined &&
                (!Number.isSafeInteger(upload.maxSize) || upload.maxSize <= 0))
                throw new Error('maxSize must be a positive safe integer.');
        }
        return { ...entry, accessPath, actions, uploadOne, uploadMany };
    });

原文件 SHA-256：7d47a56116ad446611f1a4cc765097770c92c87fcfa1e11209dfab80f4306524

E2 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:115–188

上传路由先解析 principal 与 exposure policy、拒绝 create:false，再经 bodyLimit 与 multipart 校验写入，201 返回装饰 contentUrl 的记录：权限判定先于请求体读取。

                for (const action of ['uploadOne', 'uploadMany']) {
                    const config = entry[action];
                    if (config === undefined)
                        continue;
                    // Permission comes first: the principal and the exposure's `create` Policy are decided before the size
                    // limit, the content type or the multipart body is looked at, so a refused caller learns nothing about its
                    // input and nothing is ever written to storage on its behalf. The Repository that step binds reaches the
                    // handler through the request's own Context, which every handler in the chain shares.
                    const authorized = new WeakMap();
                    const maxSize = config.maxSize ?? (action === 'uploadOne' ? 5 : 20) * 1024 * 1024;
                    router.post(`/${entry.name}/${action}`, async (c, next) => {
                        // The exposure's own Policy governs an upload, so a Policy that reads the principal has to be built
                        // here rather than when the router was.
                        let policy;
                        if (typeof entry.policy === 'function') {
                            const principal = await options.principal?.(c);
                            if (principal === undefined || principal === null)
                                throw uploadError('PERMISSION_DENIED', 'PRINCIPAL_REQUIRED', 'This endpoint requires a principal and none was resolved.');
                            policy = entry.policy(principal);
                        }
                        else {
                            policy = entry.policy;
                        }
                        // The Repository would refuse the row too, but only after the object had been stored and then removed
                        // again. The reason is the Repository's own, so a client sees the same refusal either way.
                        if (policy.create === false)
                            throw new ApiError({
                                status: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
                                reason: 'WRITE_FORBIDDEN',
                                domain: appErrorDomain,
                                message: 'create is forbidden by Policy.',
                            });
                        authorized.set(c, resolve(app, entry, policy));
                        await next();
                    }, describeUpload(entry.name, action, maxSize), bodyLimit({
                        maxSize,
                        onError: (c) => apiErrorHandler(uploadError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'BODY_TOO_LARGE', 'Upload request body is too large.', 413), c),
                    }), async (c) => {
                        const writable = authorized.get(c);
                        if (!writable)
                            throw new Error('Upload reached its handler unauthorized.');
                        if (!c.req
                            .header('content-type')
                            ?.toLowerCase()
                            .startsWith('multipart/form-data;'))
                            throw uploadError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE', 'Expected multipart/form-data.', 415);
                        let body;
                        try {
                            body = await c.req.parseBody({ all: true });
                        }
                        catch (cause) {
                            throw uploadError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'INVALID_MULTIPART', 'Invalid multipart body.', undefined, cause);
                        }
                        const value = body.file;
                        if (action === 'uploadOne') {
                            if (!(value instanceof File))
                                throw uploadError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'INVALID_FILE', 'Exactly one File is required.');
                            // Every successful upload creates a file record.
                            return c.json({
                                data: decorate(await writable.uploadOne({ file: value }), getUrl, true),
                            }, 201);
                        }
                        const uploads = Array.isArray(value)
                            ? value
                            : value === undefined
                                ? []
                                : [value];
                        if (!uploads.length ||
                            !uploads.every((file) => file instanceof File))
                            throw uploadError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'INVALID_FILES', 'At least one File is required.');
                        return c.json({
                            data: decorate(await writable.uploadMany({ files: uploads }), getUrl, true),
                        }, 201);
                    });

E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:194–227

内容路由按 uuid(.ext) 匹配，缺失或 ext 不符即 404；设置 Cache-Control: private,no-store、X-Content-Type-Options:nosniff、CSP sandbox 后流式返回字节；redirect 模式拒绝回指访问路由的存储 URL。

        defineRootRoutes((app) => {
            const router = fileRouter();
            for (const entry of entries) {
                const files = resolve(app, entry, unwritablePolicy);
                router.get(`${entry.accessPath}/:file`, async (c) => {
                    const match = /^([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12})(?:\.([a-z0-9]{1,32}))?$/.exec(c.req.param('file'));
                    if (!match)
                        return c.notFound();
                    const record = await files.findOne({ filter: { id: match[1] } });
                    if (!record || record.ext !== (match[2] ?? ''))
                        return c.notFound();
                    const disk = app.container
                        .resolve(driveManagerToken)
                        .use(record.disk);
                    if (!(await disk.exists(record.key)))
                        return c.notFound();
                    c.header('Cache-Control', 'private, no-store');
                    if (entry.accessMode === 'redirect') {
                        const url = await files.getStorageUrl(record);
                        const publicBasePath = app.publicBasePath ?? '';
                        // Check the final target after the host rewrites root-relative redirects.
                        const location = new URL(addBasePathToLocation(url, publicBasePath), c.req.url);
                        if (location.origin === new URL(c.req.url).origin &&
                            entries.some((item) => location.pathname.startsWith(`${joinBasePath(publicBasePath, item.accessPath)}/`)))
                            throw new FileRepositoryError('STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE', 'Storage URL points back to a file access route.');
                        return c.redirect(url, 302);
                    }
                    c.header('Content-Type', validMime(record.mimeType));
                    c.header('Content-Length', String(record.size));
                    c.header('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
                    c.header('Content-Security-Policy', "sandbox; default-src 'none'");
                    c.header('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename*=UTF-8''${encodeURIComponent(Buffer.from(record.filename).toString('utf8')).replace(/['()*]/g, (char) => `%${char.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase()}`)}`);
                    return c.body(Readable.toWeb(await disk.getStream(record.key)));
                });

E4 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/repository.js:7–49

FILE_COLUMNS 规定文件集合必须提供的列；uploadPolicy 由 exposure policy 派生，继承 create.scope/defaults、替换字段白名单，create:false 时上传一并被拒。

export const FILE_COLUMNS = [
    'id',
    'disk',
    'key',
    'filename',
    'ext',
    'mimeType',
    'size',
    'createdAt',
    'updatedAt',
];
/**
 * The Policy the upload path binds, derived from the exposure's own.
 *
 * It is derived rather than used as it stands because an upload supplies no
 * caller fields at all — `store()` composes every value — so a field allowlist
 * has nothing to constrain here and the plugin substitutes its own columns.
 * What is inherited is exactly the two dimensions that do not describe caller
 * input: which rows this principal may produce (`scope`), and what the server
 * stamps onto them (`defaults`). A `create` that is `false` stays `false`, so
 * an exposure that forbids creating rows forbids uploading them too.
 *
 * This is the only place a Policy is widened, and the reason is specific to
 * this path. It licenses nothing elsewhere.
 */
export function uploadPolicy(base) {
    const create = base.create === true || base.create === false
        ? base.create
        : {
            scope: base.create.scope,
            defaults: base.create.defaults,
            fields: FILE_COLUMNS,
            relations: {},
        };
    // `read` serves this path's own returning record and the public byte route,
    // both of which need every column. `update` and `delete` never run here.
    return {
        create,
        read: { scope: true, fields: FILE_COLUMNS, relations: {} },
        update: false,
        delete: false,
    };
}

E5 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/registry/component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:61–98

预览分派对 image/pdf/audio/video 直接渲染 img/iframe/audio/video，无 onError 或失败态；仅 office/OOXML 分支有失败回退（第 61-64 行的 error 分支只处理传入的 error 属性）。

  if (error)
    return (
      <div role='alert'>{t(messageKey(error), { defaultValue: error })}</div>
    );
  if (!url && kind !== 'unsupported')
    return (
      <div role='status'>
        {t('files.loadingPreview', { defaultValue: 'Loading preview...' })}
      </div>
    );
  switch (kind) {
    case 'image':
      return (
        <img
          src={url}
          alt={file.filename}
          className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full object-contain'
        />
      );
    case 'pdf':
      return (
        <iframe title={file.filename} src={url} className='h-[70vh] w-full' />
      );
    case 'audio':
      return <audio controls src={url} className='w-full' />;
    case 'video':
      return <video controls src={url} className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full' />;
    case 'markdown':
      return <MarkdownPreview text={text} />;
    case 'text':
      return (
        <pre className='max-h-[70vh] overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-md bg-muted p-3 text-sm'>
          {text ??
            t('files.loadingPreview', { defaultValue: 'Loading preview...' })}
        </pre>
      );
    default:
      return <DownloadFallback file={file} onDownload={onDownload} />;

export function FileThumbnail({
  file,
  url,
  alt = file.filename,
}: FileThumbnailProps): ReactElement {
  const imageUrl = resolveSafeFileUrl(
    url ?? (isSafeImagePreview(file) ? (file.contentUrl ?? '') : ''),
  );
  return imageUrl ? (
    <img
      data-slot='file-thumbnail'
      src={imageUrl}
      alt={alt}
      className='h-full w-full object-cover'
    />
  ) : (
    <span
      data-slot='file-thumbnail'
      aria-label={file.filename}
      className='flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-muted/50 p-3 text-muted-foreground'
    >
      {icon(file)}
    </span>
  );

E7 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:120–128

Skill 说明内容路由 "is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it"，要求 "register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it" 并禁止 use('*')，但未给出应用侧可用的挂载钩子名。

Every exposed action appears in the application's API document at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`), under the exposure's name as its tag with operationIds such as `invoiceAttachmentsFindMany` and `invoiceAttachmentsUploadOne`. The upload operations are documented as `multipart/form-data` with their `maxSize`, `201` result and `400`/`403`/`413`/`415` errors; read it there rather than guessing a request shape. The content route under `accessPath` is outside `/api` and is not in the document.

`policy` is required and governs every action of the exposure, uploads included. It is a Repository Policy: `read`, `create`, `update` and `delete`, each `true`, `false`, or a rule node. A node that is `false` refuses that operation; a node with no `fields` accepts no caller-supplied field, which is what a metadata `createOne` needs before it will accept anything. Pass a function of the principal — `policy: (principal) => ({ ... })` — together with a `principal(context)` resolver to scope rows to the caller; without a principal the request is refused with 403.

An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

Content is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.

原文件 SHA-256：c744bc1612249bfe04fbfdc8f3674a0607c13da88beb053a9b5d4fbc361de80b

export function createDriveManager(config, options = {}) {
    assertDefaultDisk(config);
    return new DriveManager({
        default: config.default,
        fakes: options.fakes,
        services: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(config.disks).map(([name, disk]) => [
            name,
            () => createDriver(disk),
        ])),
    });
}
export function assertDefaultDisk(config) {
    if (!config.disks[config.default]) {
        throw new Error(`Default drive disk "${config.default}" is not configured.`);
    }
}
function createDriver(disk) {
    if (disk.driver === 'fs') {
        return createFsDriver(disk);
    }
    return createS3Driver(disk);
}
function createFsDriver(disk) {
    return new FSDriver(compactObject({
        location: disk.location,
        visibility: disk.visibility,
        urlBuilder: disk.url
            ? {
                generateURL: async (key) => joinDriveUrl(disk.url, key),
            }
            : undefined,
    }));
}
function createS3Driver(disk) {
    const options = compactObject({
        bucket: disk.bucket,
        region: disk.region,
        endpoint: disk.endpoint,
        cdnUrl: disk.cdnUrl,
        forcePathStyle: disk.forcePathStyle,
        supportsACL: disk.supportsACL,
        encryption: disk.encryption,
        credentials: createS3Credentials(disk),
        visibility: disk.visibility,
    });
    return new S3Driver(options);
}

E14 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:243–262

Auth.optional() 返回 (context,next) 中间件，尝试设置 context 变量 auth 但不拒绝匿名请求。

    optional(options = {}) {
        return async (context, next) => {
            const originFailure = await this.checkCookieWriteOrigin(context);
            if (originFailure)
                return originFailure;
            if (options.skip?.(context)) {
                await next();
                return;
            }
            try {
                context.set('auth', await this.getSession(context.req.raw.headers));
            }
            catch (error) {
                if (error instanceof APIError)
                    return rejectedCredential(context, error);
                throw error;
            }
            await next();
        };
    }

E15 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:263–293

Auth.required() 返回中间件：无会话时以 401 UNAUTHENTICATED/AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED 拒绝，成功时 context.set('auth', session)。

    required(options = {}) {
        return async (context, next) => {
            const originFailure = await this.checkCookieWriteOrigin(context);
            if (originFailure)
                return originFailure;
            if (options.skip?.(context)) {
                await next();
                return;
            }
            let auth;
            try {
                auth = await this.getSession(context.req.raw.headers);
                // A refused credential is Better Auth's APIError; answer with its status, and its code as the reason.
            }
            catch (error) {
                if (error instanceof APIError)
                    return rejectedCredential(context, error);
                throw error;
            }
            if (!auth) {
                return apiErrorHandler(new ApiError({
                    status: 'UNAUTHENTICATED',
                    reason: 'AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED',
                    domain: 'authentication',
                    message: 'Authentication required.',
                }), context);
            }
            context.set('auth', auth);
            await next();
        };
    }

E16 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:72–78

"Every route owns its own security"：挂载到 /api 不认证任何请求，auth.required() 拒绝匿名，要求每个路由自行保护。

## Every route owns its own security

**Mounting under `/api` does not authenticate anything.** `/api` is a location. A route with no `auth.required()` is public regardless of where it mounts.

Never depend on middleware installed by another route, or on the order contributions happen to be registered in. Contribution order changes when a plugin is added, and a route protected only by someone else's middleware silently becomes public.

`auth.required()` rejects anonymous requests with `401 UNAUTHENTICATED`, reason `AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED`. `auth.optional()` attaches the session when present without rejecting.

E17 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:175–177

"Scope middleware to paths you own"：禁止顶层 router.use('*', ...)，主张显式路径或隔离子路由。

## Scope middleware to paths you own

Use the explicit path, or an isolated sub-router mounted at your prefix as above. A `router.use('*', ...)` on the top-level router leaks into contributions mounted later and is the way an unrelated endpoint accidentally becomes protected — or, worse, the way yours accidentally is not.

    async findOne(options) {
        const collection = await this.collection();
        const policy = this.readContext(collection);
        const selection = await this.validateSelect(collection, options.select, options.context, policy);
        const callerFilter = await this.normalizeFilterWithoutPolicy(collection, options.filter, options.context);
        if (!callerFilter && options.sort === undefined) {
            invalid('INVALID_FILTER', 'findOne() requires filter or non-empty sort.', {
                collection: collection.name,
            });
        }
        const filter = await this.applyReadPolicy(collection, callerFilter, policy);
        const sort = await this.validateSort(collection, options.sort, !callerFilter, policy);
        return (await this.options.adapter.findOne({
            collection,
            fields: selection.fields,
            select: selection.select,
            filter,
            sort,
            limit: 1,
        }));

    async createOne(options) {
        const policyInput = options.writePolicy === undefined ? true : options.writePolicy;
        assertWriteEnabled(policyInput);
        const writePolicy = policyInput === true ? true : normalizeWritePolicy(policyInput, 'create');
        const collection = await this.collection();
        assertWritableCollection(collection);
        // Before the payload is looked at: a Policy that forbids this operation
        // refuses the call outright, so an empty or malformed body is reported as
        // forbidden rather than as invalid input. The caller learns nothing about
        // the shape of a write they may not perform.
        resolveWriteShapeNode(this.options.policy, 'create', collection);
        await validateWritePolicyMetadata(this.options.collections, collection, writePolicy);
        const createValues = applyCreateDefaults(this.options.policy, evaluateValues(options.values));
        const mutation = await normalizeModelMutation(this.options.collections, collection, createValues.values, 'createOne', 1, { nodes: 0, clientKeys: new Set(), context: options.context });

        assertMutationWritePolicy(mutation, writePolicy, ['values'], [], 'create', collection.name);
        const selection = await this.validateSelect(collection, options.select, options.context, this.readContext(collection));
        const requestedFields = selection.fields;
        const executionFields = includeExecutionFields(collection, requestedFields);
        const result = await this.options.adapter.createOne({
            collection,
            meta: normalizeRepositoryEventMeta(options.meta, collection.name),
            fields: executionFields,
            values: mutation.values,
            relations: mutation.relations,
            select: selection.select,
            scopeCheck: await this.scopeCheck(collection, 'create'),
            relationScopes: await this.writeRelationScopes(collection, 'create'),

E21 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:78–106

nocobase-db Skill 第 2 节给出 defineMigration + builder.createCollection 的迁移写法与自包含/合并后不可变/幂等 seed 规则。

## 2. Migrations and seeds

A migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job, and a seed is never sample or demonstration content.

```ts
// database/main/migrations/202609020001_create_orders.ts
import { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202609020001_create_orders',

  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('orders', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('orderNo', { length: 64, nullable: false });
      collection.string('status', { length: 32, nullable: false });
      collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.unique('orderNo');
      collection.index('status');
    });
  },

  async down({ builder }) {
    await builder.dropCollection('orders');
  },
});

export default migration;
```

E22 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:143–170

第 3 节列出 Builder 字段与约束 API（increments/string/datetime/uuid/primary/index/unique/foreignKey 等），并强调使用逻辑名称。

## 3. Builder

`builder` is the schema API, and a migration is where it belongs. Runtime code has `db.builder()` available and should not use it: schema that changes outside the migration history is schema nothing can reproduce.

`createCollection(name, callback)` takes a callback, not a field array:

```ts
await builder.createCollection('orderItems', (collection) => {
  collection.increments('id');
  collection.integer('orderId', { nullable: false });
  collection.integer('quantity', { nullable: false, defaultValue: 1 });
  collection.foreignKey('orderId', {
    references: { collection: 'orders', fields: ['id'] },
    name: 'fk_order_items_order',
  });
  collection.index(['orderId', 'quantity']);
});
```

Field builders are `increments`, `integer`, `bigInt`, `string`, `char`, `text`, `boolean`, `decimal`, `float`, `double`, `date`, `time`, `datetime`, `datetimeTz`, `json`, `blob`, `uuid`, and `native` for a dialect-specific type. Options include `nullable`, `length`, `defaultValue`, `precision` and `scale`; the equivalent chained form (`.notNull()`, `.defaultTo()`, `.unique()`) is also available — pick one style and keep the file consistent.

Relations are `belongsTo`, `hasOne`, `hasMany` and `belongsToMany`. Constraints and indexes are `primary`, `unique`, `foreignKey` and `index`, each taking one field name or an array.

Change an existing table with explicit alter, rename and drop operations in a safe dependency order. Do not drop and recreate — that destroys data and is not what `down()` should undo.

Builder arguments are **logical names**. Physical table and column names are generated deterministically from `underscored` and `tablePrefix`; never store or concatenate `tableName` and `columnName` yourself. The exception is a raw index or constraint name such as `fk_order_items_order`, which is passed through unconverted and must be spelled the same way when dropped.

`db.builder()` is a method on the manager; `connection.builder` is a property. Getting that backwards is the most common typo in this area.

原文件 SHA-256：d33a58740d6a2921689e56f964c081354574ed857389118e319719bde6936991

E23 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/database-and-data.md:5–17

database-and-data.md 说明从容器解析 databaseManagerToken，以及 repository/query/transaction 的分工与数据访问应放在 service 的边界。

## Resolving the database

Resolve `databaseManagerToken` from the container:

```ts
import { databaseManagerToken } from '@nocobase/db';

const database = app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
```

In a route factory, resolve it once in the factory rather than inside each handler. In a service, take it as a constructor dependency — see [services and jobs](services-and-jobs.md).

From there, `database.repository(collection)` is the default for a collection's records and their relations, `database.query()` is for a result set that is not any collection's records, and `database.transaction(fn)` groups writes that must succeed or fail together. Each takes an optional connection name and uses the default connection when omitted.

export function defineAppRoutes(routes) {
    return Object.freeze({
        parent: 'app',
        routes: freezeNavigationRoutes(routes),
    });
}
function freezeNavigationRoutes(routes) {
    return Object.freeze(routes.map((route) => Object.freeze({
        ...route,
        ...(route.navigation
            ? { navigation: Object.freeze({ ...route.navigation }) }
            : {}),
        ...(route.children
            ? { children: freezeNavigationRoutes(route.children) }
            : {}),
    })));
}
export function defineSettingsRoutes(routes) {
    return Object.freeze({
        parent: 'settings',
        routes: freezeNavigationRoutes(routes),
    });
}

E26 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:120–128

file 插件 Skill 的 Registry/接入段说明组件是 App-owned 可编辑源、从 index.ts 导入、注册插件不安装/升级它，并给出上传与预览的接入方式。

Every exposed action appears in the application's API document at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`), under the exposure's name as its tag with operationIds such as `invoiceAttachmentsFindMany` and `invoiceAttachmentsUploadOne`. The upload operations are documented as `multipart/form-data` with their `maxSize`, `201` result and `400`/`403`/`413`/`415` errors; read it there rather than guessing a request shape. The content route under `accessPath` is outside `/api` and is not in the document.

`policy` is required and governs every action of the exposure, uploads included. It is a Repository Policy: `read`, `create`, `update` and `delete`, each `true`, `false`, or a rule node. A node that is `false` refuses that operation; a node with no `fields` accepts no caller-supplied field, which is what a metadata `createOne` needs before it will accept anything. Pass a function of the principal — `policy: (principal) => ({ ... })` — together with a `principal(context)` resolver to scope rows to the caller; without a principal the request is refused with 403.

An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

Content is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.

E27 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:136–140

认证 Skill 明确 "Usernames contain 3-30 letters, digits, underscores or dots"：连字符不在允许集合内，后来的 422 INVALID_USERNAME 属未遵循已写明的约束。

`Auth.pluginApi<TPlugin>(pluginId)` returns only the registered plugin’s API methods through Better Auth’s normal dispatch, including before and after hooks. It does not expose the full authentication context and does not authenticate a caller. `Auth.forConnection(connection)` binds those operations to a caller-owned transaction. Consumers still enforce authorization; HTTP-specific hooks must explicitly check for `request` or `headers` instead of accidentally denying trusted server calls.

## Initial administrator

Before the first seed run, set `users.initialAdmin.username`, `users.initialAdmin.email` and `users.initialAdmin.password` in application configuration to customize the bootstrap account. Omitting the entire node preserves `nocobase` / `admin@nocobase.com` / `admin123`; an explicit node requires a nonempty password and defaults the username to `nocobase` and the email to `admin@nocobase.com`. Usernames contain 3–30 letters, digits, underscores or dots, emails must be valid addresses, and both are stored lowercase. Initialization only runs when the user table is empty, hashes the password and grants the newly created account root permission through the authorization plugin. After initialization, configuration changes do not reset accounts or rerun the recorded seeds. The root permission seed selects the administrator by the same configured username. Use the user administration service for subsequent account changes. Do not edit historical seeds or add a competing administrator seed.

E28 · app/server/routes/material-files.ts:21–51

应用的 exposure 把 read/create scope 与 defaults 绑到 principal.id，update/delete 关闭，仅暴露 uploadOne，并另行用中间件守护字节路由。

const exposure: FileRepositoryApiExposure<Principal> = {
  name: 'materialFiles',
  collection: 'material_files',
  disk: 'local',
  accessPath: MATERIAL_FILES_ACCESS_PATH,
  // Read scopes to the uploader; uploads stamp the uploader onto the new row, so a client can never choose an owner.
  // Update and delete stay closed here — attaching a file to a material is this application's own route.
  policy: (principal) => ({
    read: { scope: { ownerId: principal.id } },
    create: {
      scope: { ownerId: principal.id },
      defaults: { ownerId: principal.id },
    },
    update: false,
    delete: false,
  }),
  actions: { uploadOne: {} },
};

/**
 * The upload endpoint (`POST /api/materialFiles/uploadOne`).
 *
 * Authentication is enforced by the application on the whole `materialFiles` prefix, and the exposure's policy scopes
 * every row it writes to the caller. The byte route under `accessPath` is guarded separately.
 */
export const materialFileRoutes: readonly AppRouteContribution<Application>[] =
  defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes<Principal>({
    repositories: [exposure],
    principal: (context: Context) =>
      (context as Context<AuthEnv>).get('auth')?.user as unknown as Principal,
  });

/**
 * The application's materials API: list, read, create, update and delete a material and the files it owns. Every route
 * is scoped to the signed-in user by the service, so a caller never names an owner.
 */
export const materialsRoutes = defineApiRoutes<Application>((app) => {
  const router = new Hono();
  const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);
  const materials = () => app.container.resolve(materialsServiceToken);
  const present = (material: MaterialRecord) => withContentUrl(app, material);

  router.use('/materials', auth.required());
  router.use('/materials/*', auth.required());

  router.get(
    '/materials',
    describeRoute({
      tags: ['Materials'],
      summary: 'List the materials of the signed-in user',
      operationId: 'listMaterials',
      responses: {
        '200': listResponse(MaterialSchema),
        '401': apiErrorResponse(401),
        '500': apiErrorResponse(500),
      },
    }),
    async (context) => {
      const items = await run(() => materials().list(ownerId(context)));
      return context.json({ data: items.map(present) });
    },
  );

E30 · app/server/middleware/material-access.ts:28–71

应用中间件用 defineHttpMiddleware：对 /api/materialFiles/* 要求登录；对 <accessPath>/:file 读 session 并核对 ownerId，匿名 401、他人 404，隐藏记录是否存在。

export const materialAccessMiddleware = defineHttpMiddleware<Application>({
  name: '@nocobase/app/materials/access',
  register(router, app) {
    // An application assembled without the authentication plugin has nothing to guard; the routes it owns are not
    // mounted either. Skipping keeps such an assembly, which the runtime tests build, startable.
    if (!app.container.has(authenticationToken)) return;
    const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);

    router.use(
      '/api/materialFiles/*',
      auth.required() as unknown as MiddlewareHandler,
    );

    router.use(`${MATERIAL_FILES_ACCESS_PATH}/:file`, (async (
      context: Context,
      next: () => Promise<void>,
    ) => {
      const session = await readSession(auth, context.req.raw.headers);
      const ownerId = session?.user
        ? await findFileOwner(app, context.req.param('file'))
        : undefined;
      if (!session?.user || ownerId !== session.user.id) {
        return apiErrorHandler(
          new ApiError(
            session?.user
              ? {
                  status: 'NOT_FOUND',
                  reason: 'MATERIAL_FILE_NOT_FOUND',
                  domain: 'materials',
                  message: 'File not found.',
                }
              : {
                  status: 'UNAUTHENTICATED',
                  reason: 'AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED',
                  domain: 'authentication',
                  message: 'Authentication required.',
                },
          ),
          context,
        );
      }
      await next();
    }) as unknown as MiddlewareHandler);
  },

E34 · app/client/pages/materials/index.tsx:15–64

列表页用 useApiClient + PageContainer/PageHeader/Loading，含 error/empty 状态，复用模板页面外壳。

export default function MaterialsPage(): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const api = useApiClient();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [materials, setMaterials] = useState<Material[]>();
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>();
  const listVisible = /^\/materials\/?$/.test(location.pathname);

  // The list stays mounted under a covering child route, so it reloads whenever the user comes back to it.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!listVisible) return;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    void listMaterials(api, controller.signal)
      .then((items) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setMaterials(items);
        setError(undefined);
      })
      .catch((cause: unknown) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setError(
          cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : 'materials.loadFailed',
        );
      });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [api, listVisible]);

  return (
    <PageContainer>
      <PageHeader
        actions={
          <Button render={<Link to='new' />}>
            <Plus aria-hidden='true' />
            {t('materials.new')}
          </Button>
        }
        description={t('materials.description')}
        title={t('materials.title')}
      />

      {error ? (
        <p
          className='rounded-md border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/5 p-3 text-sm text-destructive'
          role='alert'
        >
          {t(error, { defaultValue: error })}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {!materials && !error ? <Loading /> : null}

E35 · app/client/extensions/nocobase-file-component-ui/components/previewers/file-preview-content.tsx:102–135

应用复制的 Registry 预览源新增 ImagePreview：onError 时显示 files.imageFailed 明确提示，正是 B06 通过所需的应用侧改动。

function ImagePreview(inputProps: {
  readonly file: FileRecord;
  readonly url?: string;
  readonly onDownload?: () => void;
}): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation('@nocobase/app-plugin-file');
  const { file, url, onDownload } = inputProps;
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  if (!url || failed) {
    return (
      <div role='alert'>
        <DownloadFallback
          file={file}
          message={t('files.imageFailed', {
            defaultValue:
              'This image could not be displayed. The file may be corrupt or in a format the browser cannot show.',
          })}
          onDownload={onDownload}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <img
      src={url}
      alt={file.filename}
      className='max-h-[70vh] max-w-full object-contain'
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
}

E36 · app/client/layouts/use-sidebar-preference.ts:19–41

模板的 useSidebarPreference 通过 localStorage + 自定义 DOM 事件共享状态，setCollapsed 同步 dispatchEvent；点击后断言可能早于 React flush。

function subscribe(notify: () => void): () => void {
  const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {
    if (event.key === storageKey || event.key === null) notify();
  };
  window.addEventListener('storage', onStorage);
  window.addEventListener(changeEvent, notify);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('storage', onStorage);
    window.removeEventListener(changeEvent, notify);
  };
}

function setCollapsed(update: SetStateAction<boolean>): void {
  const current = getSnapshot();
  memoryValue = typeof update === 'function' ? update(current) : update;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(storageKey, String(memoryValue));
  } catch {
    memoryOnly = true;
    // The toggle remains usable when storage is blocked.
  }
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(changeEvent));
}

Content that renders inside another page — an inline child route, a tab panel — already sits in\n * that page's container and adds none of its own, while a covering `RouteChildPage` is a surface of its own and\n * places one inside it.\n */\nexport function PageContainer({\n  className,\n  ...props\n}: PageContainerProps): ReactElement {\n  return (\n    <section\n      className={cn('w-full space-y-6 p-6 md:p-8', className)}\n      {...props}\n    />\n  );\n}\n===== page-header =====\nimport type { ReactElement, ReactNode } from 'react';\n\nexport interface PageHeaderProps {\n  readonly title: ReactNode;\n  readonly description?: ReactNode;\n  /** Page-level actions, aligned to the right of the title from the `sm` breakpoint up. */\n  readonly actions?: ReactNode;\n}\n\n/** The heading of a page: its only `h1`, an optional description, and the actions that apply to the whole page. */\nexport function PageHeader({\n  actions,\n  description,\n  title,\n}: PageHeaderProps): ReactElement {\n  return (\n    <header className='flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between'>\n      <div className='min-w-0'>\n        <h1 className='font-heading text-3xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.035em]'>\n          {title}\n        </h1>\n        {description ?
