Because it covers rather than replaces, the page beneath\n * keeps its DOM — a draft being typed, a scroll position — and gets it back when this layer closes.\n *\n * Unlike the other two it is deliberately **not** modal. It does not portal out of the content area or trap focus,\n * because the user is still on a page of the application and must be able to reach the sidebar. What closes it is\n * the `BackButton` above its heading, or the browser's back button — not an X or Escape.\n *\n * It is two elements, the way the layout's content area is: the outer one positions and never scrolls, the inner one\n * scrolls. A deeper layer rendered beside this component anchors to the content area, as this one does, which is\n * where a page's own outlet belongs. One rendered inside it — a child page of a tab, which can only render through\n * the tab's outlet — anchors to the outer element: it covers this layer whole instead of scrolling out of sight with\n * the content once the user has scrolled this page.\n */\nexport function RouteChildPage({\n  children,\n  className,\n}: RouteChildPageProps): ReactElement {\n  const enclosingScrollerRef = useContext(EnclosingLayerContext);\n  const layerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const scrollerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  // Covering a page is not the same as closing it: the siblings underneath keep their DOM, and with it their place\n  // in the tab order and the accessibility tree. Left alone they are reachable behind opaque paint — a keyboard\n  // user tabs through controls nothing on screen shows, and focusing one scrolls the covered page into view while\n  // the layer, anchored to the content area, does not move. `inert` switches off exactly what this layer covers.\n  // The sidebar and the header are outside the content area and so outside this list, which is what keeps the\n  // component non-modal. A sibling already marked belongs to the layer below and is left for it to restore.\n  //\n  // Inside another layer, what this one covers also surrounds it: the enclosing page's header and tab bar, up to that\n  // layer's scrolling element. That element stays live, since it holds this layer; everything else in it is covered.\n  // At this layer's own level only the siblings before it are covered: the ones after it are deeper layers.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const layer = layerRef.current;\n    const boundary = enclosingScrollerRef?.current;\n    const covered: Element[] = [];\n    const cover = (element: Element): void => {\n      if (element.hasAttribute('inert')) return;\n      element.setAttribute('inert', '');\n      covered.push(element);\n    };\n    for (\n      let sibling = layer?.previousElementSibling ?? null;\n      sibling;\n      sibling = sibling.previousElementSibling\n    ) {\n      cover(sibling);\n    }\n    if (layer && boundary?.contains(layer)) {\n      for (\n        let ancestor = layer.parentElement;\n        ancestor && ancestor !== boundary;\n        ancestor = ancestor.parentElement\n      ) {\n        for (\n          let sibling = ancestor.previousElementSibling;\n          sibling;\n          sibling = sibling.previousElementSibling\n        ) {\n          cover(sibling);\n        }\n        for (\n          let sibling = ancestor.nextElementSibling;\n          sibling;\n          sibling = sibling.nextElementSibling\n        ) {\n          cover(sibling);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    return () => {\n      for (const element of covered) element.removeAttribute('inert');\n    };\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={cn(\n        'absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden bg-background',\n        className,\n      )}\n      data-slot='route-child-page'\n      ref={layerRef}\n    >\n      {/* Scrolling stops at this layer rather than carrying on into the page it covers, which keeps that page's\n          scroll position for when this layer closes. */}\n      <div\n        className='h-full overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain'\n        ref={scrollerRef}\n      >\n        <EnclosingLayerContext.Provider value={scrollerRef}>\n          {children}\n        </EnclosingLayerContext.Provider>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n===== use-route-overlay =====\ncat: client/components/use-route-overlay.tsx: No s

1) {\n      const edge = x === y || x + y === width - 1;\n      raw[offset] = edge ? 0x1f : 0xe0;\n      raw[offset + 1] = edge ? 0x6f : 0xf0;\n      raw[offset + 2] = edge ? 0xd6 : 0xff;\n      offset += 3;\n    }\n  }\n  return Buffer.concat([\n    Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a]),\n    chunk('IHDR', ihdr),\n    chunk('IDAT', zlib.deflateSync(raw, { level: 9 Defaults to the parent route with the current query string, as closing a route overlay does, so\n   * the list a child page covers keeps its search and filters.\n   */\n  readonly to?: To;\n  /** The label, \"Back\" by default. */\n  readonly children?: ReactNode;\n  readonly className?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * The way back from a page that sits below another one: a covering child page, a record's own page, a form too long\n * for a dialog.\n *\n * It stands on its own, above the page's heading where breadcrumbs would otherwise be, and needs no `PageHeader`.\n * Going back is navigation, so it is a muted text link with an arrow, turning to the foreground on hover: no button\n * chrome, so it reads as a way out rather than an action. It replaces the history entry, as closing a route overlay\n * does, so the browser's Back does not return to the page just left, such as a form that would reopen empty.\n */\nexport function BackButton({\n  children,\n  className,\n  to,\n}: BackButtonProps = {}): ReactElement {\n  const { t } = useTranslation();\n  const location = useLocation();\n\n  return (\n    <Link\n      replace\n      className={cn(\n        'inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground',\n        className,\n      )}\n      to={to ?? { pathname: '..', search: location.search }}\n    >\n      <ArrowLeftIcon className='size-4' />\n      {children ?? t('navigation.back', { defaultValue: 'Back' })}\n    </Link>\n  );\n}\n===== route-child-page.tsx =====\nimport {\n  createContext,\n  useContext,\n  useEffect,\n  useRef,\n  type ReactElement,\n  type ReactNode,\n  type RefObject,\n} from 'react';\n\nimport { cn } from '@/lib/utils';\n\nexport interface RouteChildPageProps {\n  readonly children?: ReactNode;\n  readonly className?: string;\n}\n\n// The scrolling element of the layer a child page is rendered inside, when it is rendered inside one.\nconst EnclosingLayerContext =\n  createContext<RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null> | null>(null);\n\n/**\n * A child page, laid over the page that opened it.\n *\n * It is the third way a child route can present itself, beside `RouteDialog` and `RouteDrawer`: those float in the\n * middle or at the side, this one covers the content area. Because it covers rather than replaces, the page beneath\n * keeps its DOM — a draft being typed, a scroll position — and gets it back when this layer closes.\n *\n * Unlike the other two it is deliberately **not** modal. It does not portal out of the content area or trap focus,\n * because the user is still on a page of the application and must be able to reach the sidebar. What closes it is\n * the `BackButton` above its heading, or the browser's back button — not an X or Escape.\n *\n * It is two elements, the way the layout's content area is: the outer one positions and never scrolls, the inner one\n * scrolls. A deeper layer rendered beside this component anchors to the content area, as this one does, which is\n * where a page's own outlet belongs. One rendered inside it — a child page of a tab, which can only render through\n * the tab's outlet — anchors to the outer element: it covers this layer whole instead of scrolling out of sight with\n * the content once the user has scrolled this page.\n */\nexport function RouteChildPage({\n  children,\n  className,\n}: RouteChildPageProps): ReactElement {\n  const enclosingScrollerRef = useContext(EnclosingLayerContext);\n  const layerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const scrollerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  // Covering a page is not the same as closing it: the siblings underneath keep their DOM, and with it their place\n  // in the tab order and the accessibility tree. Left alone they are reachable behind opaque paint — a keyboard\n  // user tabs through controls nothing on screen shows, and focusing one scrolls the covered page into view while\n  // the layer, anchored to the content area, does not move. `inert` switches off exactly what this layer covers.\n  // The sidebar and the header are outside the content area and so outside this list, which is what keeps the\n  // component non-modal. A sibling already marked belongs to the layer below and is left for it to restore.\n  //\n  // Inside another layer, what this one covers also surrounds it: the enclosing page's header and tab bar, up to that\n  // layer's scrolling element. That element stays live, since it holds this layer; everything else in it is covered.\n  // At this layer's own level only the siblings before it are covered: the ones after it are deeper layers.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const layer = layerRef.current;\n    const boundary = enclosingScrollerRef?.current;\n    const covered: Element[] = [];\n    const cover = (element: Element): void => {\n      if (element.hasAttribute('inert')) return;\n      element.setAttribute('inert', '');\n      covered.push(element);\n    };\n    for (\n      let sibling = layer?.previousElementSibling ?? null;\n      sibling;\n      sibling = sibling.previousElementSibling\n    ) {\n      cover(sibling);\n    }\n    if (layer && boundary?.contains(layer)) {\n      for (\n        let ancestor = layer.parentElement;\n        ancestor && ancestor !== boundary;\n        ancestor = ancestor.parentElement\n      ) {\n        for (\n          let sibling = ancestor.previousElementSibling;\n          sibling;\n          sibling = sibling.previousElementSibling\n        ) {\n          cover(sibling);\n        }\n        for (\n          let sibling = ancestor.nextElementSibling;\n          sibling;\n          sibling = sibling.nextElementSibling\n        ) {\n          cover(sibling);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    return () => {\n      for (const element of covered) element.removeAttribute('inert');\n    };\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={cn(\n        'absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden bg-background',\n        className,\n      )}\n      data-slot='route-child-page'\n      ref={layerRef}\n    >\n      {/* Scrolling stops at this layer rather than carrying on into the page it covers, which keeps that page's\n          scroll position for when this layer closes. */}\n      <div\n        className='h-full overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain'\n        ref={scrollerRef}\n      >\n        <EnclosingLayerContext.Provider value={scrollerRef}>\n          {children}\n        </EnclosingLayerContext.Provider>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n===== page-container.tsx =====\nimport type { ComponentProps, ReactElement } from 'react';\n\nimport { cn } from '@/lib/utils';\n\nexport type PageContainerProps = ComponentProps<'section'>;\n\n/**\n * The frame of a page: the full width, the responsive padding, and the spacing between its sections.\n *\n * A page renders one. Content that renders inside another page — an inline child route, a tab panel — already sits in\n * that page's container and adds none of its own, while a covering `RouteChildPage` is a surface of its own and\n * places one inside it.\n */\nexport function PageContainer({\n  className,\n  ...props\n}: PageContainerProps): ReactElement {\n  return (\n    <section\n      className={cn('w-full space-y-6 p-6 md:p-8', className)}\n      {...props}\n    />\n  );\n}\n===== page-header.tsx =====\nimport type { ReactElement, ReactNode } from 'react';\n\nexport interface PageHeaderProps {\n  readonly title: ReactNode;\n  readonly description?: ReactNode;\n  /** Page-level actions, aligned to the right of the title from the `sm` breakpoint up. */\n  readonly actions?: ReactNode;\n}\n\n/** The heading of a page: its only `h1`, an optional description, and the actions that apply to the whole page. */\nexport function PageHeader({\n  actions,\n  description,\n  title,\n}: PageHeaderProps): ReactElement {\n  return (\n    <header className='flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between'>\n      <div className='min-w-0'>\n        <h1 className='font-heading text-3xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.035em]'>\n          {title}\n        </h1>\n        {description ? Concurrent calls share one request. */\n  readonly close: () => Promise<void>;\n  /** True while a close request for the current location is pending. */\n  readonly isClosing: boolean;\n}\n\n/** Internal context shared by both route overlay components. */\nexport const RouteOverlayContext: Context<RouteOverlayContextValue | null> =\n  createContext<RouteOverlayContextValue | null>(null);\n\n/**\n * Closes the enclosing `RouteDialog` or `RouteDrawer`. Call it from a component rendered inside the overlay — its\n * body or its footer — never from the page that returns the overlay, which sits outside the provider.\n */\nexport function useRouteOverlay(): RouteOverlayContextValue {\n  const value = useContext(RouteOverlayContext);\n  if (!value) {\n    throw new Error(\n      'useRouteOverlay must be used inside RouteDialog or RouteDrawer',\n    );\n  }\n  return value;\n}\n===== route-overlay.ts =====\ncat: client/components/route-o

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

PNG 照片与 DOCX 文档内联预览

甲上传 sample.png 与 sample.docx 并保存，刷新后资料详情仍显示已保存附件；PNG 预览渲染真实图片（32×32），可下载且 SHA-256 与样例一致；DOCX 在应用内渲染出正文 Factory DOCX sample，而非只有文件名或下载按钮。

标题必填与失败后附件保留

留空标题上传 sample.docx 后保存失败并提示 A title is required.，已上传附件仍以 Done 状态保留；补上标题后一次保存成功，无需重新上传。

私有资料与附件的访问隔离

普通同事乙的资料列表为空，打开甲的资料显示 Material 2 was not found.，附件直链返回 404 MATERIAL_FILE_NOT_FOUND；未登录访问资料链接跳转登录，附件返回 401；甲仍可正常查看自己的资料与附件。

损坏文件明确失败提示

损坏 PNG 上传后被接受，但预览弹窗明确提示 This image could not be displayed. The file may be corrupt or in a format the browser cannot show.，未把只显示文件名当作预览成功。

A1 · 5 条操作 · 4 条观察 · 1 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

甲上传 PNG 并保存资料，刷新后能看到真实图片，并能下载打开。

实际操作

  1. 以资料员甲(materials.owner)登录，打开示例资料「海淀改造验收资料」详情
  2. 通过 Choose files 上传 fixtures/sample.png，状态显示 sample.png: Done
  3. 点击 Save 保存，随后 reload 页面
  4. 点击 Preview: sample.png 打开预览，检查 img 的 naturalWidth/naturalHeight
  5. 点击 Download: sample.png 下载，用 sha256sum 与样例清单校验和比对

观察结果

  1. 保存并刷新后资料详情仍显示已保存附件 sample.png 及 Preview/Download/Remove 按钮
  2. 预览弹窗内 img src=/main/uploads/materials/ebc168a0-...png，naturalWidth=32 naturalHeight=32，为真实图片内容
  3. 下载文件 99 字节，sha256=eafcdecc1468f7b18a15a7d22c494ab7e14a435f0560798ad463efc5e14f3490，与 sample.png 清单校验和一致
  4. 旧会话首次下载曾返回 Download was canceled（会话残留），按规则用同会话 close 后重新打开、重新登录复测，下载正常并校验通过

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

标题等未修改字段保持原值 朝阳项目立项资料

A4 · 4 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 1 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

移除一个附件并保存，刷新后这份资料不再显示该附件。

实际操作

  1. 以资料员甲登录，打开「朝阳项目立项资料」详情，此时已保存 sample.png 与 sample.docx
  2. 点击 Remove: sample.png 移除该附件
  3. 点击 Save 保存，然后 reload 页面
  4. 检查资料详情中剩余附件列表

观察结果

  1. 移除并保存后刷新，资料「朝阳项目立项资料」只剩 sample.docx，sample.png 不再出现
  2. 标题等未修改字段保持原值 朝阳项目立项资料

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

05

乙和未登录访问者分别打开甲的资料或附件链接，均不能看到内容；甲仍可正常查看。

甲重新登录后仍可正常查看 /main/materials/2 的两个附件

A5 · 5 条操作 · 5 条观察 · 6 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

乙和未登录访问者分别打开甲的资料或附件链接，均不能看到内容；甲仍可正常查看。

实际操作

  1. 甲登录后取得资料链接 /main/materials/2 与附件链接 /main/uploads/materials/ebc168a0-...png
  2. 退出登录，以未登录状态打开甲的资料链接与附件直链，观察响应
  3. 以普通同事乙(materials.colleague)登录并完整重新加载应用，查看 Materials 列表
  4. 乙直接打开 /main/materials/2 与附件直链，观察响应
  5. 重新以甲登录打开 /main/materials/2 确认仍可查看

观察结果

  1. 未登录打开甲资料链接被重定向到 /main/login
  2. 未登录请求附件内容返回 401 UNAUTHENTICATED，无图片内容
  3. 乙的 Materials 列表显示 No materials yet，看不到甲的资料
  4. 乙打开甲资料链接显示 Material 2 was not found.，打开附件直链返回 404 MATERIAL_FILE_NOT_FOUND，均无内容
  5. 甲重新登录后仍可正常查看 /main/materials/2 的两个附件

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

06

使用损坏 PNG，上传或预览实际发现问题时给出明确失败提示；上传已拒绝时不必继续预览。

预览弹窗明确提示 This image could not be displayed. The file may be corrupt or in a format the browser cannot show.，未把只显示文件名当作预览成功

A6 · 3 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 1 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

使用损坏 PNG，上传或预览实际发现问题时给出明确失败提示；上传已拒绝时不必继续预览。

实际操作

  1. 以资料员甲登录，打开「朝阳项目立项资料」详情
  2. 上传 fixtures/corrupt.png，观察上传状态
  3. 点击 Save 保存后点击 Preview: corrupt.png，读取预览弹窗提示

观察结果

  1. corrupt.png 上传未被拒绝，状态显示 Done（进入下一步预览）
  2. 预览弹窗明确提示 This image could not be displayed. The file may be corrupt or in a format the browser cannot show.，未把只显示文件名当作预览成功

来源：verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

完整原始验收要求
B01. 甲上传 PNG 并保存资料，刷新后能看到真实图片，并能下载打开。

B02. 甲上传 DOCX 并保存，刷新后在应用内看到文档正文，而非只有文件名或下载按钮。

B03. 先上传文件但留空标题，保存应失败且已选附件仍保留；补上标题后能直接保存。

B04. 移除一个附件并保存，刷新后这份资料不再显示该附件。

B05. 乙和未登录访问者分别打开甲的资料或附件链接，均不能看到内容；甲仍可正常查看。

B06. 使用损坏 PNG，上传或预览实际发现问题时给出明确失败提示；上传已拒绝时不必继续预览。
查看原始验收摘要与逐条数据
